Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一框架，通过可解释的稀疏扰动（XA-L₀惩罚）诊断黑箱分类器的误分类原因并评估其鲁棒性，同时引入容忍区域混淆矩阵（TOR-Confusion Matrix）量化类别间转移概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时实现误分类诊断和鲁棒性评估，缺乏统一的可解释性框架来处理黑箱分类器中的这两个关键问题。

Method: 构建优化目标函数，包含XA-L₀惩罚（促进稀疏可解释修改）和分类器损失（引导扰动样本指向目标标签），并定义TOR-Confusion矩阵衡量容忍扰动下的类间转移概率。

Result: 在图像和表格数据集上验证了该方法能够同时提供可解释性和鲁棒性评估，有效诊断误分类原因并量化分类器脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为黑箱分类器的误分类诊断和鲁棒性评估提供了一种兼具可解释性和实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a unified approach for diagnosing misclassification and assessing the robustness of black-box classifiers. Central to our method is an optimization framework that modifies an instance so that the classifier predicts a specified target label, while ensuring that the modification remains easily explainable. The objective function contains two components: an explainability-aware $L_0$ (XA-$L_0$) penalty that promotes sparse and interpretable modifications, and a classifier loss objective that steers the perturbed instance toward the desired output. This integrated optimization formulation is used both to identify the underlying causes of misclassification and to evaluate robustness by determining how an instance can change within a tolerance region before being reassigned to another class. To quantify robustness, we introduce the Tolerance Region Confusion Matrix (TOR-Confusion Matrix), which measures a classifier's susceptibility by modeling the class-to-class transition probabilities induced by tolerance-bounded perturbations. We validate the proposed method on both image and tabular datasets, demonstrating its ability to jointly deliver interpretability and robustness assessment.

</details>


### [74] [UASPL: Uncertainty-Aware Self-Paced Learning with Evidential Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06638)
*Yifan Zhang,Yuxin Hu,Zhuobin Hao,Xiaozhuan Gao,Lipeng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据神经网络的不确定性感知自步学习方法（UASPL），通过将预测可靠性融入样本选择过程，解决了传统自步学习中小损失样本未必可靠的问题，在多个数据集上取得了更好的分类性能、可解释性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自步学习（SPL）依赖损失函数值从小到大地选择样本，但损失值小的样本预测结果不一定可靠，不一定是简单样本。因此需要将预测可靠性引入样本选择机制。

Method: 基于证据神经网络，在主观逻辑框架下设计了一个通用损失函数，该函数融合了不确定性估计，并将样本选择偏好耦合其中，形成了UASPL方法，可扩展到不同的SPL变体。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，UASPL在分类性能、可解释性和泛化性方面均优于其他自步学习方法。

Conclusion: UASPL通过不确定性感知机制有效提升了自步学习的样本选择质量，增强了模型的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-paced learning (SPL) is an effective learning paradigm that simulates the human learning process by progressing from easy to difficult samples based on the value of the loss function during the learning process. It has shown great potential in improving model performance and training efficiency. However, the prediction results of samples with smaller loss values are not necessarily reliable, indicating that such samples are not always simple samples for the model. Hence, this article proposes an uncertainty-aware self-paced learning based on evidential neural networks, termed UASPL, which integrates predictive reliability into sample selection through a general loss function within the Subjective Logic framework. This loss function incorporates uncertainty estimation and can be extended to different variants of SPL. Moreover, this loss function couples a sample selection preference, thereby ensuring the interpretability of the sample selection process. Finally, the experimental results on multiple datasets show that UASPL outperforms other SPL methods in terms of classification performance, interpretability, and generality. The source code is available at: https://github.com/treelife979/UASPL.

</details>


### [75] [At-Grok Is Not Converged:A Measurement-Validity Audit for Grokking Representation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06639)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络在模算术任务中的嵌入压缩现象，发现压缩滞后于泛化转换，且网络结构（如LayerNorm）显著影响压缩时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为网络嵌入压缩与泛化同步发生，但本文发现压缩实际滞后泛化转换数万步，且不同架构（MLP vs Transformer）的表现差异巨大，需要重新审视压缩与泛化的因果关系。

Method: 通过测量有效秩（effective rank）追踪嵌入压缩动态，对比MLP与Transformer在模算术任务上的表现；使用单变量消融实验（添加LayerNorm）分析滞后大小的决定因素；设计审计流程分离压缩起始与泛化转换，排除边界细胞和未收敛参考底板的干扰。

Result: 1) 压缩滞后泛化转换至少10,000步；2) MLP上有效秩高估收敛值3-5倍，Transformer上高估1.3-1.5倍；3) 添加LayerNorm将groking步的压缩比例从0.87降至0.25；4) MLP特有的深度定律（norm预算与收敛底板的关系）在Transformer上失败，且在自由权重衰减下符号反转。

Conclusion: 嵌入压缩并非泛化的即时伴随现象，而是滞后过程；LayerNorm通过非尺度不变机制显著改变压缩时间尺度；现有测量方法可能产生误导性结论，需采用本文提出的审计流程。

Abstract: On modular arithmetic, a network's embedding keeps compressing for tens of thousands of steps after it has already generalized. Reading effective rank at the grokking transition overstates the converged value by 3-5x on an MLP, and by 1.3-1.5x on a transformer trained to convergence; on the MLP it also erases which cells compress at all. Compression lags the accuracy transition by an amount on the order of the time-to-grok, at least 10,000 steps, rather than coinciding with it. A one-variable ablation shows what sets the lag size: adding LayerNorm to an otherwise identical transformer moves the fraction of compression done by the grok step from 0.87 to 0.25, and a pre-registered control rules out scale invariance as the mechanism. We package this as an audit that separates onset from compression, flags censoring, excludes boundary cells that never fully generalize, and checks that the reference floor has plateaued, with an adversarial suite that caught a false-confidence bug in our own branch. A secondary, MLP-specific depth law linking norm budget to converged floor fails a generality test on a transformer and flips sign under free weight decay. Code and the toolkit are released.

</details>


### [76] [The Rank-One Corner: How Much Value Equivalence Does a Task Need from a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06640)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 世界模型并非整体好坏，其潜变量能捕捉多少任务所需的结构（即闭包），完全取决于训练目标的维度：标量奖励目标只能安装一维投影，而高维目标则能安装对应维度的预测方向，价值等价本质上是维度性的而非全有或全无。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估世界模型的方式（如重建观测、预测奖励）过于笼统，认为模型质量是整体性的。作者质疑这种观点，认为任务实际需要的只是少数关键预测坐标（闭包），而模型能学到多少闭包取决于训练目标的维度，而非模型容量或观测数据。

Method: 在DreamerV3框架上设计受控环境（已知真实闭包），通过线性探测测量潜变量与闭包之间的可恢复结构（R²）。系统性地改变训练目标的维度（从标量到四维），比较不同目标下潜变量中闭包信息的含量，并进行容量匹配对照和原位压力测试排除其他解释。

Result: 标量奖励目标仅安装一维闭包投影（R²从0.10提升到0.76），将目标维度从1扫到4时，辅助头恰好安装对应数量的预测方向。模型自身的价值头呈现相同阶梯（幅度衰减但秩相同）。在闭包可逐帧观测的闭环任务中，重建损失足以安装结构，标量目标也足够——目标维度决定潜变量表示内容的边界，恰好出现在低成本信号无法恢复结构的区域。

Conclusion: 价值等价不是全有或全无，而是维度性的：单奖励目标是其秩一特例，模型能安装多少任务结构完全取决于它被要求预测的目标的维度。这揭示了目标维度与潜在表示内容之间的基本规律，对世界模型的设计有重要指导意义。

Abstract: A learned world model is usually judged by how faithfully it reconstructs its observations or predicts reward, as though quality were something the model simply has or lacks. But what a task actually needs from a model is narrower: the few predictive coordinates its queries depend on, which we call the closure. We show that how much of that closure a latent comes to represent is set not by the model's capacity or its observations but by the dimensionality of the objective it is trained against, and we measure this directly on a DreamerV3 stack in a controlled environment with known ground-truth closure. An aligned scalar value signal -- the objective at the heart of value equivalence -- installs only a one-dimensional projection of a closure that needs several dimensions: read through a single linear probe, the recoverable structure rises from R^2=0.10 to 0.76 as the scalar is replaced by the full objective. Sweeping the objective's dimensionality from one to four installs exactly that many predictive directions through an auxiliary head, and the same staircase appears -- at attenuated magnitude but the same rank -- through the model's own value head, so the dissociation is dimensional rather than an artifact of head form. Capacity-matched comparisons and in-situ pressure checks rule out the obvious alternatives. The law governs a regime, and we measure its boundary: on a companion closed-loop task whose structure is observable frame by frame, reconstruction installs that structure and the scalar objective suffices -- the objective decides what a latent represents exactly where cheaper training signals cannot already recover it. Value equivalence is thus not all-or-nothing but dimensional: the familiar single-reward objective is its rank-one corner, and a model installs as much of a task's structure as the objective it is asked to predict.

</details>


### [77] [The Approximation Ratio for the Risk of Myopic Bayesian Active Learning for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06642)
*Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了主动学习中的贪心算法，首次证明了其在风险上的近似比（与最优算法相比），该比值与最大初始杠杆分数（MILS）线性相关，并通过数值模拟验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习回答“应该选择哪些数据来观测？”这一基本问题，而贪心算法虽常用但其理论保证尚不充分。

Method: 建立了贪心算法在最优实验设计中的近似比，该比值紧致到绝对常数，并引入MILS作为关键量化指标。

Result: 首次证明了贪心算法在风险上的近似比，该比值与MILS线性相关且紧致到绝对常数。

Conclusion: 贪心算法在主动学习线性回归中具有可证的理论保证，MILS是影响其性能的核心因素。

Abstract: Active learning studies the fundamental question: what data should we choose to observe? The greedy algorithm in optimal experiment design is a common heuristic and also equivalent to myopic Bayesian active learning for linear regression, the common framework where long-term planning is replaced with the one-step optimal choice. In this work, we prove a first-of-its-kind approximation ratio for the greedy algorithm's risk that is tight up to an absolute constant. The approximation ratio is linear in the maximum initial leverage score (MILS), a newly identified quantity fundamental to the greedy algorithm's performance. Finally, we illustrate the results with simple numerical simulations.

</details>


### [78] [Diffusion enabled Optimal Transport distances for graph matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06646)
*Iman Seyedi,Francesco Archetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散半松弛融合Gromov-Wasserstein (DsrFGW) 方法，通过最优传输统一节点特征和图结构，利用扩散过程增强图比较的鲁棒性，在36个合成图匹配任务中显著优于传统方法，尤其在高噪声和中等难度场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gromov-Wasserstein及其半松弛变体在处理稀疏、有噪声或部分观测的图时表现不佳，亟需一种更鲁棒的图比较方法。

Method: 受图扩散距离启发，DsrFGW将扩散过程融入半松弛融合Gromov-Wasserstein框架，允许信息在节点间传播，从而捕捉局部和全局结构模式，降低对噪声或缺失边的敏感度。

Result: 在36个合成图匹配任务（简单、中等、困难）中，DsrFGW相比srFGW准确率提升0-20个百分点；中等难度下srFGW的ARI为负值（不如随机），而DsrFGW在内外聚类质量上均显著更优。即使在严重噪声下，DsrFGW在92%的任务中提升聚类质量。

Conclusion: DsrFGW建立了一种鲁棒的图比较框架，在结构不确定性条件下表现出色，最佳扩散尺度能自适应问题难度。

Abstract: This paper introduces Diffusion Semi-Relaxed Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (DsrFGW), a novel method for graph comparison that unifies node features and structural connectivity through optimal transport. While traditional Gromov-Wasserstein and semi-relaxed variants (srGW, srFGW) capture graph structure, they often struggle with sparse, noisy, or partially observed graphs. Inspired by Graph Diffusion Distance, which posits graphs are similar if they enable similar information transmission patterns, DsrFGW incorporates diffusion processes allowing information propagation across nodes, capturing local and global structural patterns while reducing sensitivity to noise or missing edges. An extensive evaluation on 36 synthetic pairwise graph matching tasks (easy, medium, hard) demonstrates consistent superiority over srFGW, achieving accuracy improvements of 0-20 percentage points and dramatic Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) gains: in medium-difficulty scenarios, srFGW often achieves negative ARI (worse than random) while DsrFGW offers better performance in terms of both internal and external clustering quality measures (i.e., Adjusted Rank Index and Accuracy with respect to the true underlying clusters, respectively). Even under severe noise, DsrFGW improves clustering quality in 92% of the synthetic tasks with optimal diffusion scales adapting to problem difficulty, establishing DsrFGW as a robust framework for graph comparison under structural uncertainty.

</details>


### [79] [Final Checkpoints Are Not Enough: Analyzing Latent Reasoning Faithfulness Along Training Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06648)
*Hengyu Jin,Shu Yang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文追踪了潜在推理方法在训练过程中忠实度的演化，发现其因果贡献随训练衰减，且忠实度依赖于训练阶段和答案格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究在收敛检查点发现潜在推理存在不忠实行为（如推理步骤可被替换而不改变答案），但未考察这些行为在训练过程中如何形成。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对输入施加可验证的反事实编辑，并对潜在推理步骤进行噪声消融激活补丁，在多个保存的检查点上追踪不同潜在推理范式的忠实度演化。

Result: (i) 输出层面，不同潜在推理方法在收敛时表现相似的不忠实，但演化轨迹不同；(ii) 激活层面，潜在推理步骤对最终答案的因果贡献随训练衰减，且输出层面翻转的样本恰好是贡献衰减的样本；(iii) 激活层面轨迹因答案格式而异，在二元选择上衰减，在开放式解码上上升。

Conclusion: 潜在推理的忠实度依赖于训练阶段和答案格式，不能仅凭收敛时的表现判断其忠实性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning methods perform multi-step inference entirely in the model's continuous hidden states, promising more compact and efficient reasoning. However, these opaque hidden states raise a question of faithfulness: whether these latent reasoning steps causally drive the final answer. Prior work investigates this question at converged checkpoints and reports several unfaithful behaviors, such as latent reasoning steps that can be replaced without changing the answer, but leaves how these behaviors form during training unexamined. We instead track how faithfulness evolves across saved checkpoints for different latent reasoning paradigms, applying a verifiable counterfactual edit on the input and a noise-ablation activation patch on the latent reasoning steps. We find that (i) at the output level, latent reasoning methods can look similarly unfaithful at convergence under counterfactual edits while following qualitatively divergent trajectories; (ii) at the activation level, the causal contribution of latent reasoning steps to the final answer decays across training for both paradigms, with the examples that flip on the output side in (i) also being the examples on which this contribution decays; and (iii) the activation-level trajectory diverges by answer format, decaying on binary choice and rising on open-ended decoding. These findings highlight that latent reasoning faithfulness depends on training stage and answer format.

</details>


### [80] [Entropy-Guided Tensor Compression for Multimodal Federated Learning on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06651)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MESH-FL提出了一种基于熵引导的矩阵乘积状态（MPS）更新压缩框架，用于模态异构的联邦学习，在资源受限设备上实现了高达56.8倍的压缩率，同时最终准确率超过未压缩的FedAvg基线最多2.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习压缩方案通常对所有层和设备采用统一策略，忽略了不同模态在频谱结构和可压缩性上的差异，无法有效适配异构客户端。

Method: MESH-FL通过截断奇异值分解估计每层更新的谱熵，并根据熵值自适应地在层、模态和设备间分配MPS压缩秩，在每客户端有效载荷预算下优化压缩策略。

Result: 在15节点异构树莓派4/5集群上，MESH-FL实现最高56.8倍压缩，最终准确率超过FedAvg基线2.01%，收敛所需总传输数据量减少最多66倍。

Conclusion: 谱熵越高需要的重构秩越高，本文提出的熵引导分配方法能求解凸代理秩分配问题，在精确有效载荷模型下保持单调性，并实现带有显式压缩相关误差项的收敛保证。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) over mobile and edge devices increasingly involves multimodal models in which clients differ in both sensing capability and computational capacity. Existing update compression schemes typically apply uniform policies across layers and devices, without accounting for modality-specific differences in spectral structure and compressibility. We propose MESH-FL, an entropy-guided matrix product state (MPS) update-compression framework for modality-heterogeneous FL on resource-constrained devices. MESH-FL estimates the spectral entropy of each layer-wise update via truncated singular value decomposition and allocates MPS compression ranks adaptively across layers, modalities, and devices under per-client payload budgets. We show that higher spectral entropy necessitates a higher reconstruction rank under the majorization order on singular-value energy distributions. Building on this result, we prove that the proposed entropy-guided allocation solves a convex surrogate rank-allocation problem, preserves monotonicity under the exact payload model, and achieves convergence with an explicit compression-dependent error term. Experiments on a 15-node heterogeneous Raspberry Pi~4/5 cluster with modality-heterogeneous clients show that MESH-FL achieves up to $56.8\times$ compression while surpassing the uncompressed FedAvg baseline in final accuracy by up to 2.01%, and reduces total transmitted data to reach convergence by up to $66\times$.

</details>


### [81] [From Jumps to Signatures: a Generative Method for Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06652)
*Niels Cariou-Kotlarek,Vasileios Lampos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sigTPP，首个基于签名方法的时序点过程生成模型，通过引入跳跃路径到连续路径的稳定嵌入，实现序列级训练与评估，在多项指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗路径签名方法无法直接应用于跳跃路径（如事件序列），且现有神经TPP模型缺乏全局序列级损失函数和分布差异度量工具。

Method: 提出间隔嵌入（interarrival embedding），将跳跃路径稳定、单射地提升为有界变差连续路径；基于此构建sigTPP生成模型，使用路径级损失训练；推导三种计数路径分布差异度量。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，sigTPP在八个互补指标中取得最佳平均排名，在64%的指标-数据集对中达到或超过最强基线，相对分数上平均提升至少19%。

Conclusion: sigTPP有效将签名方法扩展到事件序列，路径级训练和分布差异度量提升了生成模型性能与评估严谨性。

Abstract: Rough path signatures are a universal feature map for continuous paths and, via the expected signature, characterise path distributions. These guarantees do not directly extend to cadlag paths of Temporal Point Processes (TPPs), limiting the use of signature methods for event sequences. Furthermore, neural TPP models, including recent generative approaches, optimise per-event objectives with no global sequence-level loss, while evaluation of variable-length event sequences lacks distributional discrepancy measures. This paper proposes a common pathwise framework for addressing these limitations. We introduce the interarrival embedding, a stable, injective lift from jump paths to continuous paths of bounded variation, extending signature methods to discrete event sequences. Our theoretical contributions give rise to sigTPP, the first signature-based generative model for TPPs, trained using a path-level loss on complete trajectories. We further analyse the space of counting paths and derive three distributional discrepancies, providing mathematically justified tools for evaluating generative TPP models. Across synthetic and real-world datasets, sigTPP achieves the best average rank based on eight complementary metrics, outperforms or is within a standard error of the strongest baseline in 64% of the dataset-metric pairs, and according to a relative score, improves against every baseline by at least 19% on average.

</details>


### [82] [Dual Attention Heads for Personalized Federated Learning in ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06653)
*Kien Le,Joseph Lindley,Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedDualAtt方法，通过将Transformer注意力头拆分为全局和本地分支，解决联邦学习中ECG数据异构性问题，在ECG分类任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中不同医疗机构的心电图(ECG)数据存在固有异构性，给稳健分类带来重大技术挑战。

Method: 提出FedDualAtt个性化联邦学习方法，将Transformer注意力头拆分为全局分支（通过FedAvg聚合捕捉跨站点共享模式）和本地分支（保持客户端特异性以适应机构级记录特征）。

Result: 在心血管疾病检测联邦学习基准FedCVD上的实验表明，FedDualAtt在ECG分类任务中优于现有FL和个性化FL方法。全局-本地头比例分析显示不同客户端受益于不同级别的架构个性化。

Conclusion: FedDualAtt通过灵活分配全局与本地注意力头，有效平衡了跨站点共享知识学习与客户端个性化适应，为异构ECG数据的联邦分类提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across institutions without sharing sensitive patient data. However, the inherent heterogeneity of electrocardiogram (ECG) data across healthcare providers presents significant technical challenges for robust classification. We propose FedDualAtt, a personalized federated learning approach that splits transformer attention heads into global and local branches. Global heads are aggregated via FedAvg to capture shared cross-site patterns, while local heads remain client-specific to adapt to institution-level recording characteristics. Experiments on FedCVD, an FL benchmark for cardiovascular disease detection, demonstrate that FedDualAtt outperforms existing FL and personalized FL methods in ECG classification tasks. Analysis of global-local head ratios reveals that different clients benefit from varying levels of architectural personalization.

</details>


### [83] [Robust Human-AI Complementarity under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06656)
*Yewon Byun,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究人类与AI预测之间的误差相关性结构如何影响人类决策者从AI预测中获取互补价值的能力，发现负相关误差结构能够保证预期效用的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型常被设计为辅助而非替代人类决策者，但实践中人类决策者往往无法实现预期的互补增益，因此需要探究影响人机互补决策的关键因素。

Method: 理论上分析人类与AI之间关于信息质量的不对称信息如何影响互补价值提取，重点关注预测误差相关性结构；并在真实世界预测基准数据上进行实证验证。

Result: 当AI的预测误差与人类决策者的预测误差呈负相关时，决策者可以构建稳健的策略，保证预期效用的提升。

Conclusion: 误差相关性结构是人机互补决策的关键因素，负相关的误差结构为人类决策者提供了可靠的互补增益路径。

Abstract: Machine learning models are often intended to augment rather than replace human decision makers, by providing information that is complementary to human judgement.
  Yet, in practice, human decision makers routinely fail to realize such complementary gains, even when models provide useful signal.
  In this work, we study how asymmetric information about the quality of information available to a human decision maker vs. an AI impacts the ability of a decision maker to extract complementary value from AI predictions.
  We show that a key factor is the error correlation structure between human and AI predictions. In particular, when the AI's prediction errors are \textit{negatively correlated} with those of the human, the decision maker can construct robust strategies which guarantee improvements in expected utility. We empirically investigate whether these conditions for complementarity arise in practice, using real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [84] [Trees from Marginals: Autoregressive drafting with factorized priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06763)
*Yuma Oda,Ryan Mathieu,Roman Knyazhitskiy,Artur Chakhvadze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weaver是一种轻量级自回归适配器，通过从因子化草稿模型的top-K边际构建提议树，恢复条件依赖关系，结合优化的CUDA内核，在SGLang中实现4.37倍的自回归解码加速，比DFlash基线提升24.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中因子化草稿模型的独立性假设导致接受率随预算增长急剧下降，需要恢复条件依赖关系以提升效率。

Method: 提出Weaver，一种轻量级自回归适配器，从因子化草稿模型的top-K边际构建提议树；为Gated Delta Net层推导无回滚的树验证算法，并在SGLang中实现优化的CUDA内核。

Result: 实现4.37倍自回归解码加速，比DFlash基线提升24.7%。

Conclusion: 通过模型与系统协同设计，Weaver有效解决了因子化草稿模型的独立假设问题，显著提升了推测解码效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding greatly increases the interactivity of autoregressive language models by trading off computation for extra tokens generated in a single forward pass. Factorized draft models are especially efficient because they predict future-token marginals in parallel, but their independence assumption causes acceptance rates to degrade sharply as the speculative budget grows. We analyze this limitation and introduce Weaver, a lightweight autoregressive adapter that constructs proposal trees from the top-K marginals of a factorized drafter. Weaver restores conditional dependencies between proposed tokens while avoiding a full-vocabulary projection. To support fast verification for models with Gated Delta Net layers, we derive a rollback-free tree-verification algorithm and implement optimized CUDA kernels in SGLang. By combining these model and systems contributions we achieve a 4.37-fold speedup over autoregressive decoding, and outperform a highly optimized DFlash baseline by 24.7%.

</details>


### [85] [Efficient Long-Horizon Learning for Learned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06772)
*Xiaolong Huang,Benjamin Thérien,James Harrison,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Efficient Long-Horizon (ELO)学习算法，通过重新分配元训练计算资源和解耦渐进式专家监督，有效解决了学习优化器在长周期内问题上的元训练效率低下和性能不足的问题，在语言建模和图像分类任务上优于AdamW并与Muon竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习优化器的元训练方法存在两大困难：(1)无法高效扩展到长周期内问题；(2)常常无法超越可比的hand-designed优化器。

Method: 提出ELO算法，核心包括：(1)将冗余的元训练计算重新分配到更长的失败区间，实现高效长周期学习；(2)强制解耦的渐进式专家监督，提供稳定的元学习信号并提升泛化能力。

Result: 在下游语言建模（GPT-2-124M/350M on FineWeb）和图像分类（ViT-B/16, ResNet-50 on ImageNet-1K）任务上，ELO显著提升了基础学习优化器的长展开性能和分布外泛化能力。ELO-Celo2在所有评估任务上一致优于调优的AdamW，在语言建模上与Muon竞争力相当。所有ELO基线元训练仅需不到7个H100 GPU小时。

Conclusion: ELO是一种高效且实用的元训练算法，能够以极低的计算成本训练出性能优于AdamW、接近Muon的学习优化器，为学习优化器的大规模应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learned optimization aims to improve upon hand-designed optimizers (e.g., Adam and Muon) by meta-learning small neural network optimizers over a distribution of tasks. While recent work has greatly advanced the architectural design and inductive biases of learned optimizers (LOs), current meta-training approaches still suffer from two main difficulties: (1) they cannot efficiently scale meta-training to long-horizon inner problems and (2) they often fail to surpass comparable hand-designed optimizers. To address these limitations, we propose Efficient Long-hOrizon (ELO) learning, an efficient meta-training algorithm that (1) reallocates redundant meta-training compute to longer failure regimes, achieving efficient long-horizon learning, and (2) enforces decoupled progressive expert supervision, providing stable meta-learning signals that additionally improve the generalization of LOs. Our empirical study evaluates ELO for meta-training both element-wise and matrix-based LOs. Across downstream language modeling (GPT-2-124M/350M on FineWeb) and image classification (ViT-B/16, ResNet-50 on ImageNet-1K) tasks, ELO substantially improves the long-unroll performance and out-of-distribution generalization of the base LOs. In particular, ELO-Celo2 consistently outperforms well-tuned AdamW across all evaluated tasks, while remaining competitive with Muon on language modeling. \textit{Notably, all ELO baselines require less than 7 H100 GPU-hours for meta-training.}

</details>


### [86] [Efficient Bayesian Deep Ensembles via Analytic Predictive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06776)
*Sina Aghaee Dabaghan Fard,Marie Maros,Jaesung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯深度集成方法，通过低维集成表示、闭式贝叶斯聚合和独立集成训练三个关键设计，实现了可解释、高效且具备校准不确定性的预测回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度集成方法缺乏可解释性和校准的不确定性估计，且计算成本高昂。本文旨在结合贝叶斯推断的统计严谨性与深度集成的可扩展性，提升模型的可解释性、计算效率与不确定性校准能力。

Method: 三个关键组件：(i) 低维集成表示：用少量训练的神经预测器组合表达预测，计算成本取决于集成规模而非数据集大小；(ii) 闭式贝叶斯聚合：通过贝叶斯线性回归组合集成预测，得到可解释的后验权重和校准的不确定性，无需近似推断；(iii) 独立集成训练：分别训练多个神经网络，产生多样化的预测表示，增强鲁棒性和不确定性校准。

Result: 在标准回归基准上，该方法取得了有竞争力的预测性能，并在不同设置下保持了可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，提供了可解释性和校准的不确定性估计，适用于独立预测及更广泛的学习系统组件。

Abstract: We introduce an efficient Bayesian deep ensemble method for predictive regression designed to enhance interpretability while maintaining competitive predictive performance and computational efficiency. Our method combines the statistical rigor of Bayesian inference with the scalability of deep ensembles, providing calibrated uncertainty estimates that enable its use not only for standalone prediction but also as a component within broader learning systems. To achieve these goals, our work relies on three key design components: (i) low-dimensional ensemble representation: predictions are expressed as a combination of a small number of trained neural predictors, enabling scalable inference whose cost depends on ensemble size rather than dataset size; (ii) closed-form Bayesian aggregation: ensemble predictions are combined using Bayesian linear regression, yielding interpretable posterior weights and calibrated uncertainty without approximate inference; and (iii) Independent ensemble training: multiple neural networks are trained separately, producing diverse predictive representations that improve robustness and uncertainty calibration. Empirical results on standard regression benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive predictive performance while maintaining reliable uncertainty estimates across settings.

</details>


### [87] [On Explicit Super-Expressive Approximation for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06781)
*Feng-Lei Fan,Ze-Yu Li,Chen-Yu Wang,Jian-Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用中国剩余定理构造固定架构神经网络，首次给出了显式参数界与逼近误差之间的非渐近量化关系，实现了对Lipschitz和Hölder光滑函数的最优逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前固定架构的神经网络虽然展现了超强表达能力，但缺乏参数大小与逼近误差之间的定量、非渐近刻画，无法明确参数界如何随误差变化。

Method: 引入中国剩余定理作为构造性编码机制，设计宽度为max{D,4}、深度为5的网络逼近Lipschitz函数；对于Hölder光滑函数，设计宽度max{2D, D+5N+1}、深度r+9的网络，并推导出参数界的显式上界。

Result: 对于Hölder光滑函数，参数界满足log₂P = O(ε^{-2D/(r+γ)} log(1/ε))，这是与参数有界-架构无界范式对偶的结果。

Conclusion: 中国剩余定理为固定架构神经网络的显式参数界分析提供了有效工具，实现了参数有界与架构固定双重约束下的非渐近逼近理论。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the fixed-architecture neural network approximation with explicit parameter bounds and elementary activations. While prior work demonstrated super-expressive approximation using fixed-size networks, they lack quantitative and non-asymptotic characterizations of parameter magnitude with respect to the approximation error. We resolve this issue by introducing the Chinese Remainder Theorem as a constructive encoding mechanism. For Lipschitz continuous functions on $[0,1]^D$, we construct a width-$\max\{D,4\}$, depth-$5$ network with explicit parameter-error trade-offs. For Hölder-smooth functions in $C^{r,γ}_A\left([0,1]^D\right)$, our fixed network of width $\max\{2D,\ D+5N+1\}$ and depth $r + 9$ achieves the parameter magnitude $\mathcal{P}$ bounded by $\log_2 \mathcal{P}=\mathcal{O}\bigl(\varepsilon^{-2D/(r+γ)}\log(1/\varepsilon)\bigr)$. This is the dual result compared to those in the parameter-bounded and architecture-unbounded paradigm.

</details>


### [88] [Enhancing deep learning models for time series classification via knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06796)
*Javidan Abdullayev,Maxime Devanne,Jonathan Weber,Germain Forestier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识蒸馏（KD）在时间序列分类（TSC）中的有效性，使用三种架构（FCN、Inception、ConvTran）在UCR数据集上进行实验，结果表明KD对中等复杂度的学生模型最有效，其中FCN参数减少38倍，Inception减少42%参数并保持相近性能，2个注意力头的ConvTran提升最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析等领域取得显著成功，但高计算和内存需求限制了其在资源受限环境中的部署。知识蒸馏作为一种模型压缩技术，可以在保持性能的同时获得更小的学生模型，但其在时间序列分类任务中的有效性尚需系统研究。

Method: 选取三种代表性架构（传统FCN、卷积Inception、基于Transformer的ConvTran），通过修改卷积滤波器、Inception模块和注意力头等架构组件，在UCR Archive基准数据集上评估知识蒸馏对不同复杂度学生模型的效果。

Result: 知识蒸馏对中等复杂度的学生模型最有益；FCN学生模型参数减少38倍；Inception学生模型以42%更少的参数达到与教师几乎相同的性能；2个注意力头的ConvTran学生模型通过蒸馏获得最显著的提升。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏在时间序列分类任务中有效，尤其适用于中等复杂度的学生模型，为模型压缩和资源受限环境下的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved remarkable success in various domains including time series analysis, computer vision and natural language processing. However, high computational and memory demands of state-of-the-art architectures pose challenges for deployment in resource-limited environments. Knowledge Distillation (KD) addresses this by transferring knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller, more efficient student model while maintaining competitive performance. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of KD for Time Series Classification (TSC) across three architectures: the classical Fully Convolutional Network (FCN), the convolutional Inception model and the transformer-based ConvTran model. We evaluate our approach on UCR Archive, the largest benchmark repository of time series datasets, by modifying architectural components such as convolutional filters, Inception modules and attention heads across the three architectures. Our results consistently show that KD most effectively benefits student models of intermediate complexity across all three architectures, with the distilled FCN student reducing parameters by a factor of 38, the distilled Inception student achieving nearly the same performance as the teacher with 42% fewer parameters and the distilled ConvTran student with 2 attention heads showing the most significant improvement through distillation. To encourage further research and reproducibility, we provide our implementation at https://github.com/MSD-IRIMAS/KD-4-TSC.

</details>


### [89] [Geometric Self-Distillation for Reasoning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06855)
*Josip Jukić,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSD提出了一种几何自蒸馏目标函数，通过Hellinger损失和Fisher-Rao距离近端项来抑制特权上下文自蒸馏中教师-学生不一致导致的分布外推理漂移，在数学推理任务上将OOD准确率平均提升5.7-8.6个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权上下文自蒸馏中，教师能看到完整线索而学生不能，导致教师对某些token过度自信，蒸馏过程中教师-学生分歧越大拉动力越强，这种累积漂移会损害模型的分布外推理能力。

Method: 提出GeoSD目标函数，包含两个互补项：(1) Hellinger损失，根据学生与教师已有的重叠程度缩放教师偏好，削弱学生尚不能支持的token上的拉动力；(2) 近端项，以Fisher-Rao距离衡量学生预测相对于近期检查点的漂移并加以惩罚。整个更新在下一token分布的几何空间中进行自然梯度下降。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，三个模型家族（1.7B-32B）中，GeoSD保持自蒸馏的分布内收益，同时将平均OOD准确率提升5.7-8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 标准蒸馏在OOD上失败是因为它通过与教师达成一致来排空高熵状态下的替代方案，导致对错误答案的自信一致性；而GeoSD能保持这些替代方案的可及性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is a practical post-training recipe for large language models, supplying dense teacher supervision on the student's own trajectories. In privileged-context self-distillation, teacher and student are the same model conditioned on the same prefix, but the teacher also sees a hint or the full solution trace. This makes supervision abundant but harder to trust: the teacher can be confident about continuations its privileged view makes obvious but the student cannot yet justify. The distillation pull is strongest where teacher and student disagree most, and over many updates it accumulates into drift that degrades out-of-distribution (OOD) reasoning. We introduce GeoSD, a geometric self-distillation objective that treats this drift as movement in the student's predictive behavior and counters it in two complementary ways. A Hellinger loss scales each teacher preference by the overlap the student already shares with it, attenuating the pull on tokens the student cannot yet support. Since these pulls still compound over training, a proximal term penalizes how far the student's predictions drift from a recent checkpoint, measured as a Fisher-Rao distance. Both are distances in the same geometry of next-token distributions, and a natural-gradient update takes its steps in that geometry rather than in parameter space. Across mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model families, GeoSD preserves the in-distribution gains of self-distillation while improving average OOD accuracy by 5.7-8.6 points over the base model, with gains holding across model scales from 1.7B to 32B. Analyzing why standard matching fails out of distribution, we find it wins agreement with the teacher by draining mass from alternatives at high-entropy states, resulting in confident agreement on wrong answers, whereas GeoSD keeps those alternatives in reach.

</details>


### [90] [What Predicts Correctness in Text-to-SQL? A Selective-Prediction Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06799)
*Robert Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI生成SQL查询的正确性不确定性评估，发现基于验证的信号（如LLM法官和集成方法）优于传统自一致性信号，能达到0.82 AUROC，而微调验证器在跨模式泛化上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在评估AI生成SQL查询的正确性时，缺乏有效的置信度信号，尤其在复杂多表场景下，需要识别哪些信号能可靠区分正确与错误的查询。

Method: 在BIRD和Spider基准上，系统比较了黑盒信号（字符串、结构、执行自一致性、模式相关性、可执行性）、白盒对数概率、基于LLM法官的验证信号以及微调验证器，使用AUROC和校准误差等指标评估。

Result: 自一致性信号AUROC约0.61-0.68；LLM法官达0.72-0.78；双提供商集成达0.82 AUROC且校准良好（ECE 0.03）；微调验证器在分布内达0.77-0.79，但在未见模式上降至0.66。

Conclusion: SQL正确性不确定性依赖基于推理的信号。微调验证器是良好的域内工具，但跨模式泛化仍需依赖大型冻结推理模型，而非微调。

Abstract: Evaluating uncertainty in AI-generated SQL queries requires estimating whether a query is correct, where correct means it executes to the same result as a human-written reference. We study which signals predict correctness on hard multi-table text-to-SQL, using AUROC to measure how well each ranks correct queries above incorrect ones. On BIRD and Spider, black-box signals such as string, structural, and execution self-consistency, a schema-relevance score, and query executability all fall between about 0.61 and 0.68 AUROC, with string self-consistency strongest at 0.675; white-box log-probability is similar (0.67). The signals that move past this ceiling are verification-based: an LLM judge scores from 0.72 (GPT-4o-mini) to 0.78 (Claude). Judges from different providers make different errors, so a two-provider ensemble reaches 0.82 AUROC with a well-calibrated probability (expected calibration error 0.03) and supports useful abstention frontiers (for example, answering 27% of questions at 24% selective risk) where self-consistency offers no valid low-risk subset. The pattern holds across two benchmarks, two generators, and two judge providers. We also ask whether a verifier can be trained. Fine-tuned verifiers, both encoder and generative, reach about 0.77 to 0.79 AUROC in-distribution but fall to about 0.66 on unseen schemas; scaling to 7B, adding schema diversity, distilling a strong judge's rationales, and cross-benchmark training all fail to close that gap. Cross-schema transfer appears to track model scale and reasoning rather than fine-tuning. In practice, correctness uncertainty for text-to-SQL lives in reasoning-based signals: a fine-tuned verifier is a good in-domain tool, but a verifier that generalizes across schemas currently means a large frozen reasoning model.

</details>


### [91] [Dissociating the Internal Representations of Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07003)
*Anthony Baez,Sheer Karny,Pat Pataranutaporn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探索了大语言模型（LLMs）中的谄媚行为（sycophancy）是否在内部表征上具有不同子类型（事实型 vs. 观点型），并发现不同模型对这些子类型的表征存在统一性或差异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管谄媚行为常被视为单一行为，但其表现形式多样。作者旨在揭示这种多面性是否反映在模型内部机制中，即不同子类型的谄媚行为是否共享相同的神经表征。

Method: 将谄媚行为解耦为事实型（基于可验证主张）和观点型（基于主观信念）两种子类型，通过训练线性探针和构建引导向量（steering vectors），在一个子类型上训练后评估其在另一个子类型上的迁移表现，以此衡量表征的共享程度。

Result: 不同LLM对这两种子类型的表征方式不同：有的模型表征更统一，有的则更独立且存在因果干扰。

Conclusion: 这种解耦方法为研究复杂模型行为的表征结构提供了一个有前景的分析框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit sycophancy, where they agree with a user's statement even when incorrect. While sycophancy is often treated as a single defined behavior, it can manifest in substantially distinct ways and circumstances, raising the question of whether this multi-faceted nature is reflected in its internal mechanisms. To address this gap, we dissociate the representations of sycophancy into factual and opinion subtypes -- motivated by the distinction between verifiable claims and subjective beliefs. We train linear probes and construct steering vectors on activations of one subtype and evaluate their transfer to the other subtype to measure to what extent they share representations. We find evidence that different LLMs represent these subtypes differently, with either more unified or more distinct and causally interfering representations. This method of dissociation offers a promising framework for studying the representational structure of complex model behaviors.

</details>


### [92] [Generative Diffusion Models of Stochastic Graph Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06833)
*Yiğit Berkay Uslu,Samar Hadou,Sergio Rozada,Shirin Saeedi Bidokhti,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去噪扩散框架的条件图信号生成模型，利用新颖的U-GNN架构（推广U-Net到图结构数据），通过图神经网络参数化的反向扩散过程，直接从图拓扑和节点特征中采样条件分布，应用于股票价格预测和无线资源分配任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖繁琐的特定应用设计，往往仅回归到条件均值而非从条件分布中采样，缺乏统一的图信号生成框架。

Method: 提出U-Graph Neural Network (U-GNN)架构，通过可学习节点选择（嵌套选择矩阵）实现多分辨率编解码，图卷积在原始图上以特定步长进行，避免显式图粗化。

Result: 在股票价格预测和最优无线资源分配两个生成任务上取得了广泛的数值结果。

Conclusion: 统一了图信号条件生成问题，证明了该扩散框架跨领域的有效性。

Abstract: Sampling stochastic signals supported on a graph underlies many graph machine learning tasks, including recommender systems, forecasting in financial markets, and wireless network optimization. In these settings, the target signals are realizations of unknown conditional distributions. However, prevailing approaches rely mostly on intricate, application-tailored designs that often regress to a conditional mean instead of sampling from the conditional law. This paper unifies such problems as conditional graph signal generative modeling and tackles them with a single denoising diffusion framework. We learn a reverse diffusion process, parametrized by graph neural networks (GNNs), that draws graph signals conditioned directly on the graph topology and on node-feature side information. The reverse process is realized by a novel architecture, the U-Graph Neural Network (U-GNN), which generalizes the image-convolutional U-Net to graph-structured signals. The U-GNN performs multi-resolution encoder--decoder processing in which pooling and unpooling reduce to a learned node selection, expressed by nested selection matrices, and a zero-padded lifting of coarse signals back to the full node set. The graph convolutions are carried out on the original graph, with a stride that sets their hop reach, so the U-GNN bypasses explicit graph coarsening at every resolution. We demonstrate our method on two generative tasks: stock price forecasting and optimal wireless resource allocation, with extensive numerical results in both domains.

</details>


### [93] [LEMUR 2: Unlocking Neural Network Diversity for AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06839)
*Tolgay Atinc Uzun,Waleed Khalid,Saif U Din,Sai Revanth Mulukuledu,Akashdeep Singh,Chandini Vysyaraju,Raghuvir Duvvuri,Avi Goyal,Yashkumar Rajeshbhai Lukhi,Muhammad A. Hussain,Krunal Jesani,Usha Shrestha,Yash Mittal,Roman Kochnev,Pritam Kadam,Mohsin Ikram,Harsh R. Moradiya,Alice Arslanian,Dmitry Ignatov,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEMUR 2是一个大规模、可扩展的架构基准框架，包含超过14,000种不同架构和750,000条训练记录，支持多任务、跨平台评估，为LLM驱动的AutoML和架构泛化提供数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS基准（如NAS-Bench、NATS-Bench）覆盖的架构设计空间狭窄且任务特定，缺乏跨领域和部署感知的评估能力。

Method: 通过AST代码变异、遗传/强化学习进化、分形架构生成、LLM引导合成（包括NN-RAG系统从900多个PyTorch模块提取架构模式）生成多样架构；利用NN-VR和NN-Lite流水线在移动端和VR平台进行自动化部署和延迟基准测试。

Result: 构建了包含14,000+架构和750,000+结构化训练记录的数据集，涵盖多模态任务、图像描述、文生图、语言建模等，并提供跨平台真实设备性能元数据。

Conclusion: LEMUR 2通过链接多样化架构、任务和部署数据，为LLM微调提供数据基础，推动了可复现、数据驱动的AI设计，以及LLM驱动的AutoML和跨模态/硬件的架构泛化研究。

Abstract: Existing NAS benchmarks (e.g., NAS-Bench, NATS-Bench) cover only narrow, task-specific regions of the architectural design space and lack cross-domain or deployment-aware evaluation. LEMUR 2 introduces a large-scale, extensible framework unifying generative, evaluative, and deployment pipelines to unlock neural-network diversity. It comprises over 14,000 distinct architectures and more than 750,000 structured training records documenting model performance, hyperparameters, and task outcomes. These models were produced through AST-based code mutation, genetic and reinforcement-learning evolution, generation of fractal architectures, and synthesis guided by a Large Language Model (LLM). This includes deep models generated with the retrieval-augmented system NN-RAG, which derived and used architectural motifs from over 900 PyTorch modules extracted from public repositories. LEMUR 2 further employs NN-VR and NN-Lite pipelines for automated deployment and latency benchmarking on heterogeneous mobile and Unity-based VR platforms, providing real-device performance metadata. It spans multimodal tasks, image captioning, text-to-image synthesis, and language modeling, supporting cross-domain analysis of architectural transferability. By linking diverse architectures, tasks, and deployment data, LEMUR 2 provides the data foundation for LLM fine-tuning and coupling diverse architectural origins with large-scale, cross-platform empirical validation. This dataset defines a new basis for reproducible and data-driven AI design, advancing the emerging paradigm of LLM-driven AutoML and architectural generalization across modalities and hardware.

</details>


### [94] [A Gold-Standard Study of What Makes a Lightweight Game-Playing Agent Strong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06854)
*Nima Kelidari,Mohammadsaeed Haghi,Mahdi Salmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、与游戏无关的强化学习训练方案，通过在Gin Rummy中构建固定规则专家作为评估标杆（不参与训练），结合信任域更新、奖励设计、课程学习、热启动和最佳检查点保存等技术，使自对弈智能体对专家的胜率从30%提升至36%。实验表明模型容量并非瓶颈，而信息限制才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完美信息卡牌游戏中的强化学习智能体只能与训练对手一样强，且难以评估（对随机对手胜率超99%，对自身副本只能平局），因此需要一个固定的强专家作为评估基准。

Method: 1. 构建固定规则专家作为评估标杆（不用于训练）；2. 测试多种技术：信任域更新、精准奖励设计、递增难度课程学习、热启动、最佳检查点保存；3. 比较MLP、卷积、集合、注意力、循环等多种编码器；4. 加入NFSP和IS-MCTS基线；5. 在Leduc Hold'em上验证（可计算最优解）。

Result: 自对弈冠军对专家胜率从约30%提升至36%；奖励塑形、状态嵌入学习、模仿学习、DAgger、大语言模型对手等方法无效或成本过高；网络容量增加无法突破性能天花板，表明限制来自信息而非模型规模。

Conclusion: 提出了一种轻量级、游戏无关的训练方案，可在不依赖专家数据的情况下训练出有竞争力的智能体，适用于小型模型能处理的游戏，并已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for imperfect-information card games are only as strong as the opponents they train against, and they are hard to grade, since they beat a random opponent over 99 percent of the time and only tie copies of themselves. So we build a strong, fixed, rule-based expert for Gin Rummy and use it only as a yardstick, never for training. It beats every agent we trained 70 to 99 percent of the time. Across more than a hundred runs, we isolate what makes a lightweight agent stronger. Trust region updates, a well-aimed reward, a curriculum of tougher opponents, warm starting, and keeping the best checkpoint all help, and stacking them lifts a self-play champion from about 30 to 36 percent against the expert. Several ideas did not pay off. Short-term and longer-term reward shaping, learned state embeddings, imitation and DAgger, and a live large language model opponent were each unhelpful, too slow, or too heavy to train at scale. Comparing MLP, convolutional, set-based, attention, and recurrent encoders shows that extra capacity does little to break the ceiling, suggesting the limit is information rather than network size. We add standard baselines (neural fictitious self-play and information set Monte Carlo search) and confirm the approach carries over to Leduc Hold'em, where the optimum is computable. The result is a lightweight, game-agnostic recipe that trains competitive agents without training on the expert, for any game a small model can handle, reported with robust statistics and released as a reusable package.

</details>


### [95] [FourierQK: Spectral Preprocessing of Query-Key Projections Improves Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07478)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Transformer注意力机制的Q/K投影上进行FFT频谱预处理，通过可学习的多尺度频率滤波器显著提升字符级语言建模性能，揭示了频域全局混合相较于随机投影的独特优势，并指出了双边频谱注意力与因果频谱注意力的架构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力在字符级语言建模中效果有限，作者探索是否可以通过对Q/K投影进行频谱预处理（而非替换注意力机制本身）来提升性能，并验证频域全局混合的有效性。

Method: 对Query和Key的投影进行FFT频谱预处理（固定随机滤波、单学习频率、多学习频率），保留完整注意力分数结构，区别于FNet的替换整个注意力为傅里叶混合。

Result: 四频率学习（49,27,10,6 tokens/cycle）达到val=0.309，比标准注意力提升79%；随机正交/非正交投影无改善；因果滤波器无效；结果通过了置换验证诊断。

Conclusion: 频谱预处理提升源于频域全局混合而非度量扭曲；双边FFT核本质上是非因果的，与因果频谱注意力存在架构边界。

Abstract: FFT-based spectral preprocessing of learned query-key (Q/K) projections substantially improves transformer attention on character-level language modelling. On TinyShakespeare: a fixed random spectral filter achieves val=1.031 (Delta=+0.443); a single learned frequency at paragraph scale achieves val=0.608 (Delta=+0.867); and four learned frequencies spanning paragraph to word scale achieve val=0.309 (Delta=+1.166), a 79% reduction over standard dot-product attention. The single-frequency result is confirmed across three random seeds (mean val=0.236, std=0.019). The four frequencies converge to a near-geometric multi-scale ordering (49, 27, 10, 6 tokens/cycle) corresponding to paragraph, sub-paragraph, phrase, and word scales. The gain is specific to spectral preprocessing: random orthogonal and non-orthogonal projections of Q/K produce no measurable improvement, suggesting the benefit comes from global frequency-domain mixing rather than metric distortion. All results are verified by a shuffled-validation diagnostic against positional leakage. Causal filters (Gaussian, Mexican Hat, Morlet) do not improve over standard attention at character-level tokenisation: the bilateral FFT kernel is structurally non-causal, coupling every position to future tokens. This defines an architectural boundary between bilateral spectral attention (this paper) and genuinely causal spectral attention at word-scale tokenisation (companion paper MorletQK). This work is architecturally distinct from FNet (Lee-Thorp et al., 2021), which replaces attention with Fourier mixing of token embeddings. Here, spectral preprocessing applies only to Q/K projections while the full attention score structure is preserved.

</details>


### [96] [Best-Arm Identification with Generative Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06879)
*Tianyi Ma,Hanzhang Qin,Ruihao Zhu,Jierui Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE算法，在固定置信度最佳臂识别问题中，利用廉价代理分数（如ML/LLM预测）与昂贵奖励的相关性来降低样本复杂度，通过普通最小二乘估计残差方差并维持上界证书，实现了与已知相关性情形相当的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动决策中，每次奖励观测成本高昂，而机器学习和大语言模型能提供廉价但相关的代理预测。如何利用这些代理信息减少高成本奖励的采样次数，同时在线学习未知的相关性，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出PROBE（PRoxy OLS for Best-arm Exploration）算法，采用阶段消除策略，通过普通最小二乘（OLS）拟合直接维护残差方差的上界证书，利用卡方分布精确性质保证证书的有效性，不受未知相关性的影响。

Result: PROBE是δ-PAC的，达到已知相关性情形的oracle样本复杂度，仅相差常数乘子和常数校准成本。该保证可扩展到(ε,δ)-PAC设置。合成数据和真实汽车贷款定价实验验证了样本节省与奖励-代理相关性强度成比例。

Conclusion: 通过控制变量调整将问题转化为异方差识别问题，残差方差(1-ρ²)决定样本节省程度。PROBE算法在在线学习未知相关性时仍能保持正确性，为利用廉价代理信息降低昂贵决策成本提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Best-arm identification is a canonical model for data-driven decision-making, but in many applications each reward observation is costly. Motivated by the growing availability of cheap predictions from machine learning and large language models, we study fixed-confidence best-arm identification in which each costly reward pull is paired with a cheap but correlated proxy score. The marginal mean of the proxy can be estimated offline and is treated as known, whereas its correlation $ρ$ with the reward, which governs how much the proxy helps, is unknown and must be learned online in pair with real rewards. We show that a control-variate adjustment turns this model into a heteroscedastic identification problem whose oracle sample complexity improves by residual variance $1-ρ^2$. The central difficulty is that the correlation must be learned from the same costly samples that identification consumes online, and that a plug-in estimate of the residual variance is anti-conservative and can compromise correctness. We propose PROBE (PRoxy OLS for Best-arm Exploration), a phase-elimination algorithm that directly maintains an upper certificate on the residual variance with an ordinary least squares fit, whose exact chi-square law keeps the certificate valid regardless of the unknown correlation. We prove that PROBE is $δ$-PAC and attains the known-correlation oracle sample complexity up to a constant multiplicative factor and a constant additive calibration cost. The guarantee extends to the $(ε,δ)$-PAC setting under minimal changes to the algorithm. Numerical experiments on synthetic instances and on an auto-loan pricing replay with large language model and tabular proxies confirm that the sample savings of PROBE scale with the strength of the reward-proxy correlation, exactly as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [97] [Max Out GRPO Signal: Adaptive Trace Prefix Control for Hard Reasoning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07674)
*Vladislav Beliaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaPrefix-GRPO通过动态调整问题前缀长度，将成功率控制在50%左右以最大化梯度信号，在硬数学问题上相比GRPO准确率提升2.1倍（0.6B模型），同时计算量减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在模型最难问题上会陷入困境：当一组rollout全部失败时，组内相对优势(advantage)为零，无法产生梯度，浪费了最需要学习的前沿样本。

Method: 将参考解的正确前缀附加到问题中，通过反馈控制器动态调整前缀长度，使成功率保持在50%附近（GRPO梯度信号最大处），训练后期逐步撤除辅助，最终模型自主解题。实现包括数据预处理和前缀token的loss mask。

Result: 在硬数学问题上，相同训练FLOPs下，0.6B模型准确率提升2.1倍，Qwen3-1.7B提升1.6倍，AIME提升1.7倍；trace长度减半；模型越小增益越大。

Conclusion: AdaPrefix-GRPO通过自适应难度控制有效解决了GRPO在困难样本上的梯度消失问题，显著提升训练效率和最终性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stalls on a model's hardest problems: when no rollout in a group succeeds, the group-relative advantages vanish and the problem contributes no gradient, wasting the frontier examples we most want to learn from. Prepending a correct prefix of a reference solution raises the success rate, making prefix length a continuous knob on difficulty. Concurrent methods set the knob once; AdaPrefix-GRPO turns it into a feedback controller: throughout training it adjusts how much of the solution each problem gets, holding its success rate near 50%, where GRPO's gradient signal is largest, then withdraws the assistance entirely, so the deployed model solves problems unaided. On hard math, at matched training FLOPs, it more than doubles GRPO's accuracy on held-out problems from the training distribution for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x on Qwen3-1.7B and 1.7x on AIME, while roughly halving trace length. The method is implemented in data preparation plus a loss mask on prefix tokens; the trainer is otherwise stock. The smaller the model, the larger the gain.

</details>


### [98] [Converge to Surprise: Evolutionary Self-supervised Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06887)
*Canlin Zhang,Xiuwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度下降、无需先验目标的自监督图像聚类框架，通过最大化"惊喜分数"来拒绝最大熵零假设，并采用进化策略与梯度下降交替优化的方法，在非参数自监督图像聚类任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督图像聚类方法都依赖梯度下降，每一步优化都需要明确的目标（如对比学习的分割、掩码、伪标签等）。作者希望打破这一限制，提出一种不需要任何先验目标和梯度信息的自监督聚类框架。

Method: 基于最大熵原理，假设每个像素i.i.d.作为零假设H0；定义"惊喜分数"衡量模型输出在H0下的不可能程度；通过最大化惊喜分数迫使模型拒绝H0，从而发现数据中的非随机特征。优化采用"收敛到惊喜"方案：外层用进化策略直接最大化惊喜分数（无需梯度），内层用梯度下降以ES发现的聚类结果作为替代目标进行周期性更新。

Result: 在标准图像基准上，该方法在非参数自监督图像聚类（最严格的深度聚类设置，不给定真实类别数）中达到了新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种不依赖梯度下降、不需要每步明确目标的自监督聚类方法，通过进化策略+梯度下降的双层优化框架，成功实现了无先验知识的自监督图像聚类，并取得了最优性能。

Abstract: Most self-supervised image clustering models, actually almost all deep learning approaches, are based on gradient descent: In order to calculate the loss, every optimization step requires a clearly defined target, whether a contrastive split, a masked patch or entity, an EMA-teacher output, a pseudo-label, or a differentiable information-theoretic functional. We propose a self-supervised framework that drops this requirement for image clustering. Without any prior knowledge, we have to assume that each pixel is i.i.d. according to the Principle of Maximum Entropy. Taking this as our null hypothesis H0, we define a "surprise score" that measures how unlikely the model's output representation would be under H0. Maximizing the surprise score forces the deep learning model to reject H0 - equivalently, to discover non-random feature from data. Also, here is our fundamental assumption: a surprise score cannot, in general, be reduced to a per-step loss. Hence, we propose the "converge-to-surprise" scheme to optimize our model: an evolution-strategy (ES) outer loop, which directly maximizes the surprise score without needing its gradient, paired with a periodic gradient-descent inner loop, which uses the surprising clusters already discovered by ES as surrogate targets. On standard image benchmarks, our framework achieves new state-of-the-art results in non-parametric self-supervised image clustering - the strictest deep-clustering setting, in which the number of ground-truth classes is not given to the model.

</details>


### [99] [Agon: Competitive Cross-Model RL with Implicit Rival Grading of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07690)
*Vladislav Beliaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agon是一种通过两个竞争模型互评答案来提升推理能力的方法，在DeepMath上比GRPO性能翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（如GRPO）只评分最终答案，不评分推理过程，导致模型在难题上倾向于写更多而非思考更好。缺乏对"好的思考"的标注。

Method: 让两个竞争力相当、行为差异化的模型相互博弈：交替扮演"起草者"和"阅读者"角色，每个模型因"比对方解决得更好"而获得奖励。通过竞争隐式地评判推理质量，无需过程标签或奖励模型。

Result: 在DeepMath困难子集上，使用Qwen3时，Agon的pass@1是GRPO的两倍，增益约为未训练的Mixture-of-Agents的8倍。该结果在编程题和多种模型族（Qwen3.5, Gemma 4）上得到验证。

Conclusion: Agon通过对抗性互评有效提升了模型的推理能力，未来方向是让模型在隐空间中进行联合推理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (e.g. GRPO) is the engine behind today's reasoning models, yet it grades only the final answer. On hard problems this trains models to write more rather than to think better, since the trace itself is never graded and no label for good thinking exists. We introduce Agon, which makes two competing models each other's graders. Both attempt the same problem; in alternating roles, one drafts a solution and the other reads it while solving, and each is rewarded for out-solving the other. To win, a model must out-reason a rival that has seen its work, so reasoning is judged implicitly during training, with no process labels and no reward model. Because both models are optimized, each faces a progressively stronger rival, which single-model RL cannot provide. The two need only be comparably strong and behaviorally different. At inference the pair deploys as it trains, a two-stage cascade in which one model drafts and the other answers after reading the draft. On the hard split of DeepMath with Qwen3, this doubles GRPO's pass@1, roughly eight times the gain of an untrained Mixture-of-Agents pass over the same base. The ordering replicates on competitive-programming code and across model families (Qwen3.5, Gemma 4). For now the models talk in text; the next step is to let them reason together in latent space.

</details>


### [100] [Latency-Constrained DNN Architecture Learning for Edge Systems using Zerorized Batch Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06922)
*Shuo Huai,Di Liu,Hao Kong,Weichen Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向延迟约束的神经网络学习方法，通过硬件定制的延迟预测器，在满足严格延迟要求的同时优化模型精度，在多个边缘设备上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的深度学习应用面临延迟约束，传统神经网络优化方法无法直接改变模型推理的时间成本，难以满足实时应用的严格延迟要求。

Method: 提出延迟导向的神经网络学习方法，引入通用硬件定制延迟预测器，通过一次性训练过程优化模型，使其在满足延迟约束的同时保持高精度。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上，GoogLeNet延迟从40.32ms降至34ms，精度仅下降0.14%；结合量化后精度仅下降0.04%。在Jetson TX2上，VGG-19从119.98ms压缩至34ms且精度提升0.5%，GoogLeNet从20.27ms扩展至34ms且精度提升0.78%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效满足硬延迟约束并实现高精度，优于现有最优方法，框架已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning applications have been widely adopted on edge devices, to mitigate the privacy and latency issues of accessing cloud servers. Deciding the number of neurons during the design of a deep neural network to maximize performance is not intuitive. Particularly, many application scenarios are real-time and have a strict latency constraint, while conventional neural network optimization methods do not directly change the temporal cost of model inference for latency-critical edge systems. In this work, we propose a latency-oriented neural network learning method to optimize models for high accuracy while fulfilling the latency constraint. For efficiency, we also introduce a universal hardware-customized latency predictor to optimize this procedure to learn a model that satisfies the latency constraint by only a one-shot training process. The experiment results reveal that, compared to state-of-the-art methods, our approach can well-fit the 'hard' latency constraint and achieve high accuracy. Under the same training settings as the original model and satisfying a 34 ms latency constraint on the ImageNet-100 dataset, we reduce GoogLeNet's latency from 40.32 ms to 34 ms with a 0.14% accuracy reduction on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano. When coupled with quantization, our method can be further improved to only 0.04% drop for GoogLeNet. On the NVIDIA Jetson TX2, we compress VGG-19 from 119.98 ms to 34 ms and even improve its accuracy by 0.5%, and we scale GoogLeNet up from 20.27 ms to 34 ms and achieve higher accuracy by 0.78%. We also open source this framework at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/ZeroBN

</details>


### [101] [Intrinsic-Noise Consolidation: A Doob-Barrier-Conditioned Diffusion Turns Analog Device Noise into a Continual-Learning Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06924)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在模拟神经形态硬件中，利用器件固有噪声（而非消除它）通过Doob h-变换实现突触巩固，在Split-MNIST任务上提升了10.9%的记忆保持率，并在BrainScaleS-2芯片上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将模拟神经形态硬件中通常有害的固有器件噪声转化为有益因素，用于巩固记忆而非损害精度。

Method: 将每个突触的巩固过程建模为Doob h-变换，通过条件化随机动力学使其永不越过记忆关键屏障，引入与噪声方差成正比的恢复漂移项，并在BrainScaleS-2芯片上利用可调噪声进行实验验证。

Result: 单头Split-MNIST任务中，该方法在内部最优噪声点提升10.9%保持率（p=0.004）；在BrainScaleS-2芯片上，屏障条件化方法比对照方法在先前任务上保持率高15.6点。

Conclusion: 模拟固有噪声可以转化为巩固红利，而数字加速器需消耗能量才能产生类似效果。

Abstract: On analog neuromorphic hardware, intrinsic device noise is normally an accuracy tax. We ask whether it can instead consolidate memories. We cast per-synapse consolidation as a Doob h-transform: condition each weight's stochastic dynamics on never crossing a memory-critical barrier around its consolidated value. The conditioned diffusion gains an extra drift sigma^2 d/dw log h, a restoring force amplified by the noise variance itself that diverges at the barrier. We are explicit about novelty: the anchored drift -s(w-mu) our rule also contains is not ours (the limit of OUA, MESU, and EWC), and we surrender it. We claim only the conjunction of (a) the Doob barrier-conditioning as a synaptic rule, to our knowledge unclaimed (every h-transform use we found is generative modeling, none synaptic), and (b) a falsifiable prediction: increasing intrinsic noise non-monotonically improves sequential-task retention, an inverted-U that anchored-drift methods cannot produce. We pre-registered this as a go/no-go gate; it passes. On single-head Split-MNIST (8 seeds) the rule lifts retention 10.9 points at an interior optimum (paired Wilcoxon p=0.004), while matched OU/EWC/MESU anchors are monotone. Ablating the conditioning removes the effect; the optimum tracks the barrier; the inverted-U survives a second task stream and the realization where noise enters the forward pass. We then measure the intrinsic noise on real BrainScaleS-2 silicon (additive, trial-to-trial independent, tunable via on-chip averaging) and run the rule on the chip with its noise in the training loop: barrier-conditioning retains a prior task 15.6 points better than the matched control at matched average accuracy, a stability-plasticity shift, not a net-accuracy win (single seed; retention measured, energy modelled). Intrinsic analog noise thus becomes a consolidation dividend a digital accelerator must spend energy to generate.

</details>


### [102] [Imputation Meets Clustering: Exploiting Latent Subgroup Structure for Missing Data Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06930)
*Chuyao Zhang,E Li,Taochen Chen,Yiqun Zhang,Yuzhu Ji,Shuping Zhao,Peng Liu,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAGI框架，通过聚类与插补的协同优化，在缺失数据场景下实现保留子群结构的生成式插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插补方法忽略了真实数据中多子群的异构性，导致边界模糊、实例级保真度低；而引入子群信息面临聚类依赖完整数据、插补又依赖子群识别的循环依赖困境。

Method: 提出"分区-引导-恢复"策略，动态聚类分配作为局部先验条件化GAN，通过迭代反馈循环协同优化聚类结构与插补值，并采用多层级优化目标（实例级重建+分布级正则化）。

Result: 在14个基准数据集上对比15种基线方法，实验结果表明CAGI显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CAGI有效解决了聚类与插补的循环依赖问题，通过协同优化实现了保留子群分布的高保真缺失数据插补。

Abstract: Missing data is prevalent in practical applications, making effective imputation an essential preprocessing step for downstream analysis. Real-world datasets often exhibit complex latent structures composed of multiple subgroups with distinct distributions. However, existing methods often overlook such population heterogeneity. Without explicit structural guidance, these methods tend to produce generic estimates that blur subgroup boundaries and lack instance-level fidelity. While incorporating subgroup information offers a remedy, it faces a circular dependency: reliable subgroup identification requires complete data, while data completion is the imputation objective itself. To resolve this, we propose CAGI (Cluster-Aware Generative Imputation), a framework that reformulates clustering and imputation as a mutually reinforcing co-optimization process. CAGI employs a ``Partition-Guide-Restore'' strategy where dynamic cluster assignments act as local priors to condition a Generative Adversarial Network. An iterative feedback loop is established to progressively refine both cluster structures and imputed values toward faithful subgroup distributions. To ensure distributional stability, CAGI further employs a multi-level optimization objective combining instance-level reconstruction with distribution-level regularization. Extensive experiments on 14 benchmark datasets with 15 representative baselines demonstrate the superiority of CAGI. The source code is available at: https://github.com/supercocachii/CAGI

</details>


### [103] [Physical activities enable scalable foundation modelling for broad-spectrum health prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06954)
*Zhenghuang Wu,Yuyao Zhu,Songlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StepFM，一种仅基于步数数据的可穿戴基础模型，用于广泛的健康风险预测。相比依赖高频传感器数据的方法，StepFM更注重隐私、计算效率和跨设备/人群的可扩展性，在20+健康风险任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感模型多为特定疾病设计，泛化能力有限；而大多数基础模型依赖高频原始传感器数据，带来隐私、计算开销和跨设备可扩展性问题。因此，需要一种更轻量、通用且保护隐私的健康预测方法。

Method: StepFM基于步数计数数据构建基础模型，设计了一个可扩展的预训练框架，从大规模步数序列中捕获时间动态和行为模式。该模型可迁移至20多种健康风险预测任务，涵盖不同设备、新地区和新型疾病。

Result: 实验表明，StepFM在多种异质设定下均达到与现有方法相当的强性能，并保持鲁棒性。同时，分析揭示了身体活动模式与多种健康风险之间的可解释、可泛化的关系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了基于步数的感知作为可扩展、真实世界健康监测的可行基础，为活动建模驱动的健康预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Wearable and mobile sensing technologies have demonstrated strong potential for health inference; however, most sensor models are designed for specific disease types, limiting their transferability across different health risks. Wearable foundation models offer a more generalizable approach in diverse health risk types. Nevertheless, most existing methods rely on high-frequency raw sensor data, raising concerns about privacy, computational overhead, and scalability across devices and populations. In this paper, we propose StepFM, a foundation model built solely on step counter data for broad-spectrum health prediction. Leveraging the ubiquity and low-dimensional nature of step data, StepFM provides a practical, privacy-preserving, and computation-efficient alternative to traditional sensor-based models. We design a scalable pre-training framework that captures temporal dynamics and behavioral patterns from large-scale step sequences, enabling transfer across more than 20 health risk prediction tasks spanning diverse devices, new regions, and novel disease types. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StepFM achieves strong performance compared to existing methods while maintaining robustness across heterogeneous settings. Furthermore, our analysis reveals interpretable and generalizable relationships between physical activity patterns and various health risks, offering new insights into activity-based health modeling. Our work establishes step-based sensing as a viable foundation for scalable and real-world health monitoring.

</details>


### [104] [Rethinking Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06973)
*Haoxin Liu,Yichen Zhou,Rajat Sen,B. Aditya Prakash,Abhimanyu Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为TimesX的多模态时间序列预测基准，包含丰富的真实世界数据和自动化生成的文本上下文，解决了现有基准泛化差、文本类型有限和数据泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模态时间序列预测基准存在三个主要问题：(1) 数据规模小且为合成数据，导致泛化能力差；(2) 基准中文本上下文类型非常有限；(3) 评估中无法缓解数据泄露问题。

Method: 构建了TimesX基准，包含高质量真实世界时间序列数据，并通过自动化数据生成管线获取多样化的文本上下文。在该基准上对零样本多模态预测方法进行了实证研究。

Result: 在现有基准上表现良好的方法在TimesX上可能会失败；而利用丰富文本上下文的简单集成方法可以在TimesX上胜过强基线方法。

Conclusion: TimesX是一个更具挑战性和现实意义的多模态时间序列预测基准，强调了文本上下文的重要性，并揭示了现有方法的泛化局限。

Abstract: We introduce a new context-enriched, multimodal time series forecasting benchmark, TimesX. TimesX contains a wide selection of high-quality real-world time series with diverse domains and textual contexts obtained from an automated data generation pipeline, which helps address three main issues of existing multimodal forecasting benchmarks: (1) poor generalization due to the small scale and synthetic nature of benchmark data, (2) very limited types of textual contexts in the benchmarks, and (3) an inability to mitigate data leakage in evaluation. We conduct a thorough empirical study of zero-shot multimodal forecasting approaches on TimesX. Our results suggest that many approaches that perform well on existing benchmarks may fail on TimesX. In contrast, simple ensemble methods that leverage rich textual context accompanying time-series can outperform strong baselines on TimesX.

</details>


### [105] [Hybrid Least Squares/Gradient Descent Methods for MIONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06976)
*Jun Choi,Chang-Ock Lee,Minam Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于MIONets的高效混合最小二乘/梯度下降（LSGD）方法，通过交替最小二乘法优化各分支网络最后一层参数，并利用Kronecker和Khatri-Rao积分解大矩阵，加速训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MIONets的训练过程，解决传统梯度下降方法在大规模参数优化时的效率问题。

Method: 将MIONet视为多个分支网络与一个主干网络的逐元素乘积之和，利用交替最小二乘法逐分支优化最后一层参数，并通过Kronecker和Khatri-Rao积以及张量置换矩阵将大矩阵分解为小矩阵，兼容带正则化项的L²损失。

Result: 提出的LSGD方法能够高效处理大尺寸系统矩阵，有效加速MIONets的训练。

Conclusion: 该混合LSGD方法推广了DeepONets的LSGD方法，为MIONets提供了一种高效的训练策略。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an efficient hybrid least squares/gradient descent (LSGD) method for MIONets to accelerate training. This method generalizes the LSGD method for DeepONets. Since MIONet is the sum of the entrywise product of multiple branch networks and a trunk network, it can be viewed as a multilinear function with respect to the last layer parameters of each branch network. These sets of parameters can be optimized using the alternating least squares method, where we solve the LS system for a single branch network in turn. To handle the large-sized system matrix, we introduce Kronecker and Khatri-Rao products and tensor permutation matrices to factor the large matrix into small ones. Our method is compatible with a general type of $L^2$ loss with regularization terms for the last layer parameters of each branch, where linear operators can be applied to the MIONet output in each loss term.

</details>


### [106] [Robust Federated Learning Under Real-World Client Churn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06979)
*Dhruv Garg,Neha Lakhani,Debopam Sanyal,Myungjin Lee,Alexey Tumanov,Ada Gavrilovska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FeLiX是一个联邦学习编排框架，通过流式感知可用性层级、新鲜效用选择和延迟鲁棒聚合三个原语，在真实部署环境中将达到目标精度的挂钟时间缩短2.37倍，同时降低1.30倍通信带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统存在刷新周期慢（多天级）的问题，导致模型无法快速适应由病毒式趋势和用户意图变化驱动的波动数据分布，影响点击率等关键指标。

Method: 提出三个原语：(i) 流式感知可用性层级——利用轻量级遥测识别就绪客户端；(ii) 新鲜效用选择——双层级机制优先选择能赶上紧刷新截止时间且有统计价值的更新；(iii) 信息感知延迟鲁棒聚合——融入包含真实结果的高价值延迟更新而不引入偏差。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Google Speech和真实低可用性轨迹上，相比最先进的同步和异步FL基线，挂钟时间缩短2.37倍，通信带宽降低1.30倍。

Conclusion: FeLiX在真实场景中实现了接近oracle性能的表现，无需依赖现实中不可用的客户端可用性先验知识，有效解决了瞬态客户端可用性、动态数据异质性和预测-结果延迟三大挑战。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables training shared models on private, on-device data, but production deployments remain constrained to slow, multi-day refresh cycles due to the complexity of coordinating massive client populations. For applications such as feed ranking, ad targeting, and personalized recommendation, model freshness: the ability to rapidly adapt to new user-local data is critical for maximizing objectives like click-through rate. This lag leaves models stale and unresponsive to volatile data distributions driven by viral trends and shifting user intent. Bridging this gap requires addressing three challenges overlooked by existing FL systems: transient client availability, dynamic data heterogeneity, and delays between model predictions and observable outcomes. We present FeLiX, an FL orchestration framework that minimizes wall-clock time-to-target accuracy on live interaction streams. FeLiX introduces three primitives: (i) streaming-aware availability tiers that leverage lightweight telemetry to identify ready clients at scale; (ii) fresh-utility selection, a dual-tier mechanism that prioritizes statistically valuable updates from devices able to meet tight refresh deadlines; and (iii) informativeness-aware, delay-robust aggregation that incorporates late, high-value updates containing ground-truth outcomes without biasing the global model toward stale distributions. Unlike prior systems that rely on unrealistic oracular knowledge of client availability, FeLiX achieves near-oracular performance in real-world settings. Across CIFAR-10, Google Speech, and realistic low-availability traces, FeLiX reduces wall-clock time-to-target accuracy by up to 2.37X while reducing communication bandwidth by 1.30X compared to state-of-the-art synchronous and asynchronous FL baselines.

</details>


### [107] [UP: Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization for Breaking the Exploration-Stability Dilemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06987)
*Chongyu Fan,Pengfei Liu,Jingjia Huang,Sijia Liu,Yi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP)，一种即插即用的RL优化目标，通过不对称设计（正优势无界梯度+负优势标准裁剪）解决探索-稳定性困境，提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL框架（如基于重要性采样的方法）面临探索-稳定性困境：纯IS导致训练不稳定，而标准裁剪机制虽能缓解不稳定却严格限制策略更新预算，抑制了探索。

Method: 提出Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP)，通过stop-gradient算子将策略锚定在当前状态，对正优势使用无裁剪的稳定梯度以最大化探索，对负优势保持标准裁剪以防止不稳定。该方法可扩展至token级（GRPO、DAPO）和序列级（GSPO）优化粒度。

Result: 在多种RL算法（DAPO、GSPO、GRPO）、模型架构（Dense、MoE、视觉-语言）和训练模式（语言、多模态）上，UP均提升了探索能力和推理准确率。

Conclusion: UP是一种通用的即插即用RL优化增强方案，有效解决了探索-稳定性困境，显著提升LLM推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become the standard paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). To achieve sample efficiency, modern RL frameworks rely on importance sampling (IS). However, these algorithms suffer from an exploration-stability dilemma. Pure IS often leads to catastrophic training instability, while standard clipping mechanisms used to mitigate this instability strictly constrain the policy update budget. By formalizing the concept of Probability Capacity (Cap), we reveal that conservative clipping structurally stifles exploration by prematurely truncating the update budget for correct but low-confidence reasoning paths. To break free from these constraints, we propose Unbounded Positive Asymmetric Optimization (UP), a universal and plug-and-play objective. UP theoretically restructures the optimization process by anchoring the policy to its current state via the stop-gradient operator. This asymmetric design unleashes unclipped, stable gradients for positive advantages to maximize exploration, while maintaining standard clipping safeguards for negative advantages to prevent training instability. Furthermore, our formulation readily extends across different optimization granularities, including token-level (GRPO, DAPO) and sequence-level (GSPO) frameworks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UP enhances exploration capacity and achieves superior reasoning accuracy across diverse RL algorithms (DAPO, GSPO, and GRPO), model architectures (Dense, MoE, and vision-language), and training modalities (language and multimodal), validating UP as a truly universal plug-and-play enhancement for RL-based training.

</details>


### [108] [Physics-guided spatiotemporal neural models for fuel density prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06999)
*Tolga Caglar,Jaynil Jaiswal,Saqib Azim,Yudhir Gala,Mai H. Nguyen,Ilkay Altintas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理引导的机器学习(PGML)框架用于燃料密度预测，通过将物理约束融入深度学习模型，在精度和稳定性上均优于纯数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动模型缺乏物理约束，预测结果可能不符合物理规律，需要将领域知识和物理约束融入深度学习模型以提高预测的准确性和稳定性。

Method: 采用ConvLSTM、AFNONet和ViViT三种深度学习架构，在损失函数中加入可微分的物理信息项（质量守恒燃料传输项和蔓延速率估计项），形成PGML框架。

Result: 在多次独立试验中，PGML框架在精度和稳定性上均优于不含物理约束的纯数据驱动基线模型。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现计算高效且物理合理的火灾预测，支持自适应 prescribed burn 管理。

Abstract: This paper presents a physics-guided machine learning (PGML) framework for fuel density prediction, integrating physics constraints and domain knowledge into deep learning models to enhance model accuracy and stability. We explore three deep learning architectures -- ConvLSTM, Adaptive Fourier Neural Operator (AFNONet), and Video Vision Transformer (ViViT) -- to model the spatiotemporal evolution of fuel density. Our approach incorporates differentiable physics-informed terms in the loss function, including a mass-conserving fuel transport term and a rate-of-spread estimation. Experimental results, averaged across multiple independent trials, demonstrate that the proposed PGML framework outperforms purely data-driven baselines without physics constraints in both accuracy and stability. This framework enables computationally efficient, physically plausible fire forecasting to support adaptive prescribed burn management.

</details>


### [109] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal-Frequency Fusion with Peak Enhancement for Cellular Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07016)
*Qingzhong Li,Yue Hu,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MSPF-Net多模态蜂窝流量预测框架，通过融合时空频率特征、突发模式与新闻上下文信息，显著提升流量预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多仅关注单一模态的内在流量模式或结构关系，难以处理真实流量中的突发性内生动<dhidden>态和外部城市事件引发的扰动，导致预测可靠性不足。

Method: MSPF-Net包含四个模块：时空频率编码器（捕获时域、空域和频域模式）、峰值增强模块（提取突发感知表示）、新闻上下文表示模块（编码城市新闻流）、动态融合预测模块（自适应整合异构信号）。

Result: 在Milano、Trento和LTE三个流量数据集上验证，联合建模流量动态、突发模式和新闻上下文信号能有效提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 引入外部上下文信息（如城市新闻）可以有效提升蜂窝网络流量预测的准确性，尤其对突发性流量变化场景效果显著。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of cellular network traffic is essential for network planning, resource allocation, and quality-of-service assurance in modern mobile communication systems. Real-world traffic often exhibits bursty endogenous dynamics and disturbances triggered by external urban events, which makes reliable prediction highly challenging. Most existing spatiotemporal traffic forecasting methods primarily focus on intrinsic traffic patterns or structural relationships within a single modality, and rarely model burst behavior together with exogenous contextual signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{MSPF-Net}, a multimodal cellular traffic forecasting framework that integrates external contextual information. Specifically, MSPF-Net consists of a Spatiotemporal-Frequency Traffic Encoder for capturing temporal, spatial, and spectral traffic patterns, a Peak Enhancement Module for extracting burst-aware representations of sudden spikes, a News Context Representation Module for encoding urban news streams into exogenous contextual embeddings, and a Dynamic Fusion Prediction Module for adaptively integrating these heterogeneous signals to generate forecasts. Experiments on the Milano, Trento, and LTE traffic datasets demonstrate that jointly modeling traffic dynamics, burst patterns, and news contextual signals can effectively improve forecasting performance.

</details>


### [110] [Online Data Selection Is Implicit Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07023)
*Aoxiong Zeng,Yuxin Yang,Xiangquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示在线数据选择本身就是一种隐式对齐机制，提出Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA)协议和Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS)方法，证明仅通过选择训练数据即可显著改变模型的行为偏好（如拒绝率、冗长度、谄媚性等），且这种偏移方向可由所选数据的属性分布预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将SFT视为能力适配步骤，而对齐归因于偏好优化或强化学习。作者认为这种分离是不完整的——在线训练中数据选择本身就会改变模型的行为偏好，需要系统研究这种隐式对齐机制。

Method: 形式化在线选择为加权SFT目标，比较随机、基于损失、基于质量和基于多样性的在线选择器；提出Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA)协议量化行为偏移，以及Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS)方法在保持数据效率的同时约束安全与风格轴上的漂移。

Result: 任务准确率上无统计差异的选择器在拒绝率、冗长度、谄媚性等行为维度上差异显著；行为偏移方向可由所选数据的属性混合分布预测。

Conclusion: 在线数据选择是一种隐式对齐机制，在线评分器扮演了通常奖励模型的角色；选择器定义的不同偏好会导致模型在安全性和风格上产生可预测的系统性偏移。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is often treated as a capability-adaptation step, while alignment is attributed to later preference optimization or reinforcement learning. This separation is incomplete: when examples are scored and kept online during fine-tuning, the choice of which data to train on already changes the model's behavioral preferences. We study online data selection as an implicit alignment mechanism. Given the same base model, optimizer, and selected-token budget, we compare random, loss-based, quality-based, and diversity-based online selectors and measure the behavioral drift they induce without any preference optimization. The proposed evaluation tracks helpfulness, refusal rate, verbosity, truthfulness, sycophancy, calibration, and jailbreak robustness, together with diagnostics for which behavioral modes are over-represented in the selected data. We formalize online selection as a reweighted SFT objective whose weights define an implicit preference over response styles and safety postures, so that an online scorer plays the role usually assigned to a reward model. This view predicts that high-scoring data can systematically favor longer, more assertive, more compliant, or more refusal-prone behaviors depending on how the online score is defined.
